It’s not on purpose… is that the kind of restlessness you mean?\n\nDoctor: Over the past few weeks or months, would you say that your frustration or discouragement has become a near daily experience, affecting your ability to get things done or enjoy your day?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I’d say near daily, no. I’ve forgotten errands I told my daughter I’d take care of. Is that the kind of memory thing you’re asking about?\n\nDoctor: Have you had any period in the last few months where you felt down or discouraged nearly every day for two weeks or more, even if it has improved by now?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so… not for two weeks straight like that. I’ve had low days, of course, and I can get discouraged with myself, but it tends to come and go. Yes… I think so, though I don’t know if I’d say it’s “thinking clearly” exactly. It’s more that I get pulled off track very easily.
